1. **Company Table**: Stores information about the company, including its name and industry.

### Explanation:
1. **Company Table**: Stores information about the company, including its name and industry.
2. **Financial Year Table**: Stores the financial year for which data is recorded.
3. **Revenue Table**: Links a company to its revenue in a specific financial year.
4. **Net Profit Table**: Links a company to its net profit in a specific financial year.
5. **Assets Table**: Links a company to its total assets in a specific financial year.
6. **Risk Table**: Tracks the risks faced by each company.

This schema ensures that all entities and relationships are properly normalized, with appropriate primary keys and foreign keys.
